So algorithm:
- Choose initial a (sqrt family or parent). Normalize b = a / sum(a) so S=1.
- At each iteration:
  c = convolve(b,b) (length 2n-1). M approx = soft_max(c; τ) or max.
  gradient of M_smooth wrt b: grad = derivative (components ~1/n).
  Update b = b - lr * grad. Clip to 0. Renormalize sum to1.
  F = 2n * max(c_actual) (or soft). Track best.
We want minimize M (since n fixed). This is unconstrained on simplex with positivity.

Implement safe convolution via FFT:
```
def convolve_full(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n < 128: return np.convolve(a,a)
    N=1
    while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:2*n-1]
    return c
```
For coordinate update, we maintain c full length L=2n-1. Updating slice:
Valid k range: j=k-i in [0,n-1] => k from i to i+n-1. But L=2n-1, k max 2n-2. Since i<=n-1, i+n-1 <=2n-2 ok. Slice k_start=i, k_stop=i+n (exclusive). j indices 0..n-1. For each position p in 0..n-1, k=i+p, j=p. We need update c[k] for all p.
If p != i: y=a[p], old=old_x, new. delta_contrib=2*(new-old)*y.
If p == i: delta=new^2-old^2.
Vectorized:
```
y = a[0:n]  # length n
contrib_change = 2*(new-old)*y
if 0 <= i < n:
    contrib_change[i] = new*new - old*old
c[i:i+n] += contrib_change
```
This works because for each p, k=i+p. For p=i, k=2i, contribution old^2 -> new^2. Good. This update is O(n) with NumPy.

Coordinate bound computation vectorized:
Given i, old=a[i], c current, a. y=a[0:n]. d? For p !=i: constraint c[i+p] - 2 old*y[p] + 2 new*y[p] ≤1. Bound = (1 - (c[i+p] - 2 old*y[p]))/(2*y[p]). For p=i: d=c[2i]-old^2, bound=sqrt(max(0,1-d)).
We can compute all bounds over length n:
```
c_slice = c[i:i+n]
d_nonself = c_slice - 2*old*y
bounds = np.full(n, np.inf)
mask = y>0
idx = np.where(mask)[0]
bounds[idx] = (1 - d_nonself[idx])/(2*y[idx])
if i < n:
   d_self = c[i+i] - old*old  (i+i <=2n-2 yes)
   self_bound = sqrt(max(0,1-d_self))
   bounds[i]=min(bounds[i], self_bound)
x_max = np.min(bounds)
# Also c constraints where y=0 no bound.
x_max = max(0, x_max)
# Due to numeric, allow x_max = max(x_max, old) if x_max < old and close? But if start feasible, x_max>=old. For safety, if x_max < old*(1-1e-9), maybe x_max=old? Or scale down? If old infeasible due to c >1, we need reduce. We can set new = min(old, x_max) if x_max<old significantly.
```

`def central_binomial_sequence(n):`
Compute coefficients C(2k,k)/4^k stably. Use recurrence: b0=1; b_{k}=b_{k-1}*(2k-1)/(2k). Because C(2k,k)/4^k = b_{k-1} * (2k-1)/(2k)? Check k=1: b0*(1/2)=0.5 yes. k=2:0.5*3/4=0.375. Good.
`def full_convolve_fft(a):` as above.
`def normalize_to_max1(a):` compute c, m=max(c); if m<=0 return None; factor=1/sqrt(m); a*=factor; c*=factor^2; return a,c.
`def coordinate_cycle(a,c,reverse=False):`
  n=len(a); S=sum(a); max_iter? We do one pass.
  if reverse: order range(n-1,-1,-1) else range(n).
  for i in order:
    old=a[i]
    if old <=0: maybe small.
    y=a  # full array
    c_slice = c[i:i+n]
    # compute d_nonself = c_slice - 2*old*y
    # But for p=i, the -2old*y includes -2old^2 though self contribution old^2; we'll handle separately. For nonself mask exclude i.
    bounds = np.full(n, np.inf)
    # Use y>1e-300
    mask = y > 0
    # For memory, we can compute bounds only where mask
    # To avoid allocating bounds full each coordinate? n up to 512 or 2000, okay. But for n=4806, maybe too slow; we won't coordinate large >2048 maybe.
    idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
    d = c_slice - 2*old*y
    valid = d < 1  # if d>1 infeasible
    # bound = (1-d)/(2*y)
    bvals = np.empty(len(idx))
    bvals[:] = (1 - d[idx])/(2*y[idx])
    # Where d >1, bound negative.
    np.minimum(bounds, bvals, out=bounds, where=mask?) Numpy `np.minimum` with out requires same shape; easier:
    bounds[idx] = bvals
    # self at p=i
    kself=2*i
    dself = c[kself] - old*old
    if dself < 1:
        selfb = math.sqrt(max(0.0, 1-dself))
    else:
        selfb = 0.0
    if selfb < bounds[i]: bounds[i]=selfb
    x_max = float(np.min(bounds))
    if not np.isfinite(x_max): x_max=old
    # If roundoff makes x_max slightly below old, allow if within tol; else decrease.
    if x_max < old:
       if old - x_max <= 1e-10 * max(1,old): x_max=old
       else: x_max=max(0.0, x_max)
    # Don't increase beyond maybe 1000 (not needed)
    if x_max > old + 1e-14:
       new=x_max
       # update c
       delta = new - old
       update = 2.0 * delta * a
       update[i] = new*new - old*old
       c[i:i+n] += update
       a[i]=new
  return sum(a)
```
But update `update = 2*delta*a` uses old a before setting a[i]; then update[i]=new^2-old^2. Good. However for p where a[p] maybe just updated earlier, that's correct.

```

Stopping for a start:
```
def coordinate_optimize(a, max_time, print_every=...):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,None)
   if len(a)==0: return None
   a,c=normalize_to_max1(a)
   if c is None: return None
   best_a=a.copy(); best_S=sum(a); best_score=2*n/(best_S*best_S)
   while time < max_time:
      S1=coordinate_cycle(a,c,reverse=False)
      S2=coordinate_cycle(a,c,reverse=True)
      a,c=normalize_to_max1(a) # returns scaled
      S=sum(a)
      if S > best_S*(1+1e-10): update best
      if abs(S-S2) < 1e-10*max(1,S): break
   return best_a
```
But `coordinate_cycle` modifies a,c and no need return S maybe compute after.

Coordinate pass details performance:
```
def coordinate_pass(a, c, reverse=False):
    n=len(a)
    order = range(n-1,-1,-1) if reverse else range(n)
    S=0.0
    for i in order:
        old=a[i]
        if old < 0: old=0; a[i]=0
        # if old is very small, still can increase
        y=a
        c_slice = c[i:i+n]
        mask = y > 1e-300
        if mask.any():
            # compute vals for masked
            num = 1.0 - (c_slice - 2.0*old*y)  # but this includes self; we can exclude later
            # Using masked arrays:
            ym = y[mask]
            num_m = num[mask]
            vals = num_m / (2.0*ym)
            # If any vals nan? no
            nonself_min = float(vals.min())
        else:
            nonself_min = math.inf
        # self correction: the masked nonself min may include p=i with incorrect bound; we need exclude i.
        # Instead of including, we can set a temporary? Simpler: compute mask_nonself = mask.copy(); mask_nonself[i]=False. But copy each i costly.
```
Need exclude self from nonself min. We can compute vals on all mask then if i included, we should ignore its value. Option: use full `vals` array length n with inf at y=0, then set vals[i]=inf temporarily? Allocating full each i okay for n<=512/1000. Simplicity:
```

mask = y > 1e-300
dm = c_slice[mask] - 2*old*y[mask]
vals[mask] = (1 - dm)/(2*y[mask])
vals[i] = np.inf
nonself_min = vals.min()
```

Use `mask = y > eps`; `idx = np.where(mask)[0]` would create arrays. Full vals simpler.

Normalize function:
```
def normalize_to_max1(a, c=None):
    if c is None: c=convolve_fft(a)
    m=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300: return None,None
    factor = 1.0 / math.sqrt(m)
    a = a * factor
    c = c * (factor*factor)
    # clean small negatives? c = np.where(c>0,c,0)? maybe not.
    return a,c
```
If m<1, scaling up; if m>1, down.

elif lowends: a = ((i+1)*(n-i)) / ((n/2)**2) + eps
elif base_window: base = central; w = ((i+1)/(n))**alpha * ((n-i)/(n))**beta; a=base*w
elif random_exp: a=np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma, n)); sigma=rng.uniform(0.2,1.5)

def coordinate_search_for_n(n, end_time, best_score_holder):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(rules)
    base = central_binomial(n)
    starts = [base.copy()]
    # generate maybe 20 random starts initially
    for _ in range(starts_per_n): starts.append(make_start(...))
    best_local_a=None; best_local_score=inf
    # active start from list; if list empty perturb best_local or random.
    while time < end_time:
       if len(starts)>0:
          a=normalize(starts.pop())
       else:
          if best_local_a is None: a=normalize(base)
          else: a=perturb(best_local_a, rng, sigma=sigma_schedule)
          a=normalize
       a,c=coordinate_optimize_slice(a,c, slice_time)
       S=sum(a); score=2*n/(S*S)
       # verify feasibility: m=c.max(); if m>1+1e-7: scale; score recompute
       if score < best_local_score:
          best_local_score=score; best_local_a=a.copy()
          if score < best_score_holder[0] - 1e-7:
              # authoritative eval
              seq=sanitize(best_local_a); sc=evaluate_sequence(seq)
              if sc < best_score_holder[0]: update global
       if time... maybe if score close to lower? break
```

```
def coordinate_optimize_slice(a, c, max_time):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    improving=True
    last_S=0.0
    no_improve_time=0
    while time < t0+max_time and time < global? pass deadline maybe:
        S_before=sum(a)
        coordinate_pass(a,c,False)
        coordinate_pass(a,c,True)
        a,c=normalize_to_max1(a,c)
        S_after=sum(a)
        if S_after > last_S*(1+1e-11):
            last_S=S_after; no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve += slice? 
        if S_after <= S_before*(1+1e-12):
            # converged; maybe perturb externally if no external start
            break
    return a,c
```

The `make_start` functions should be top-level no closures. For random smooth Gaussian:
```
def make_gaussian_start(n, rng):
    x=np.arange(n)
    mu=rng.uniform(0.1,0.9)*n
    sig=rng.uniform(0.1,0.6)*n
    a=np.exp(-((x-mu)**2)/(2*sig*sig))
    return a
```
This can have max 1, length n, scale.

Power window:
```
def make_power_window(n, rng):
    p=rng.uniform(-1.0,1.5)
    q=rng.uniform(-1.0,1.5)
    x=np.arange(n)
    left=(x+1.0)/n
    right=(n-x)/n
    a=left**p * right**q
    a=np.exp(np.log(a)) # handles? if left>0
    return np.clip(a,1e-12,1000)
```
If p negative, left 0? left starts 1/n, finite. Good.

base=central; p=rng.uniform(-1,1); q=...; left=(x+1)/n; right=(n-x)/n; w=left**p*right**q; a=base*w
```

`make_start` with kind int:
```
def make_start(n, rng, kind, best=None):
    if kind == 0: a=central_binomial(n)
    elif kind == 1: a=np.ones(n)
    elif kind == 2: geom decreasing
    elif kind == 3: geom increasing
    elif kind == 4: bell low ends
    elif kind == 5: high ends
    elif kind == 6: power window
    elif kind == 7: base power window
    elif kind == 8: random exp
    elif kind == 9: gaussian
    elif kind == 10: random smooth (base * random window?)
    elif kind >=20 and best is not None: perturb best
    else: a=np.ones(n)
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    if len(a)!=n: ...
    a,c=normalize_to_max1(a)
    if a is None: return None
    return a
```
For random exp: `rng.normal(0, sigma, n)` with sigma random; clip exponent to [-20,20]. Could have all tiny; normalize handles if max conv >0. If sum zero, skip.
For gaussian: ensure not all zero (mu/sig).
For power window: `left = (np.arange(n)+1.0)/max(1,n-1?)` use n. If p/q can be large, values can be 0 or inf; use logs:
```
loga = p*np.log(left) + q*np.log(right)
loga = np.clip(loga, -50, 50)
a=np.exp(loga)
```
For base power: base ~ <=1, similar log.

`coordinate_optimize_slice` needs `end_time` to not exceed global; pass slice and global_deadline.
```
def coordinate_optimize_slice(a,c,max_time,deadline):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < min(t_start+max_time, deadline):
        S_before=sum(a)
        coordinate_pass(a,c,False)
        if time... maybe only one pass? Do two but check.
        coordinate_pass(a,c,True)
        a,c=normalize_to_max1(a,c)
        if a is None: break
        S_after=sum(a)
        if S_after <= S_before*(1+1e-12):
            break
    return a,c
```

If coordinate_pass creates infeasible and normalize scales down, S_after may decrease, causing immediate break? But after scaling up? `sanitize_for_evaluate(a)`:
```
def sanitize_for_evaluate(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    n=len(a)
    c=convolve_fft(a)
    m=float(c.max())
    if m > 0:
        # target margin 1e-10 relative
        if m > 1.0:
            a *= math.sqrt((1.0 - 1e-10)/m)
        else:
            # we can scale up to M=1? But if a was scaled for local M=1, m maybe1. If m<1 due FFT error? We can scale up to margin to improve.
            a *= math.sqrt((1.0 - 1e-10)/m)
    # Ensure sum>=0.01
    s=np.sum(a)
    if s < 0.01:
       a = np.ones(n)*0.01/n
    # Maybe cap
    return [float(x) for x in a]
```
Scaling up if m<1 can slightly improve final score. But if m computed underestimates due FFT error, margin 1-1e-10 maybe safe? FFT error ~1e-15*condition; margin 1e-8 safer. Use `margin = 1.0 - 1e-8`. Score penalty 1e-8 negligible. If m is 0, bad.
This sanitize changes M to ~0.99999999, score approx.

Coordinate pass code:
```
def coordinate_pass(a, c, reverse=False):
    n = len(a)
    order = range(n-1, -1, -1) if reverse else range(n)
    small = 1e-15
    for i in order:
        old = float(a[i])
        if old < 0.0:
            old = 0.0
            a[i] = 0.0
        y = a
        c_slice = c[i:i+n]
        dm = c_slice - 2.0 * old * y
        with np.errstate(divide='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
            vals = (1.0 - dm) / (2.0 * y)
        mask = y > small
        if not np.all(mask):
            vals[~mask] = np.inf
        vals[i] = np.inf
        nonself_min = float(np.min(vals))
        dself = float(c[2*i]) - old*old
        if dself <= 1.0:
            self_min = math.sqrt(max(0.0, 1.0 - dself))
        else:
            self_min = 0.0
        x_max = nonself_min if nonself_min < self_min else self_min
        if not math.isfinite(x_max):
            x_max = old
        if x_max < 0.0:
            if old > 0 and (old - x_max) < 1e-8:
                x_max = old
            else:
                x_max = 0.0
        if x_max > 1000.0:
            x_max = 1000.0
        new = x_max
        if new > old + 1e-14:
            update = 2.0 * (new - old) * a
            update[i] = new*new - old*old
            c[i:i+n] += update
            a[i] = new
        elif old - new > 1e-12:
            update = 2.0 * (new - old) * a
            update[i] = new*new - old*old
            c[i:i+n] += update
            a[i] = new
```
But `update = 2.0*delta*a` uses `a` which still has old at i. Good. However for p where a[p] was updated earlier in same pass, that's correct current. For p=i, old in a; update overwritten.
Potential issue: `dm = c_slice - 2old*y` for self p=i subtracts 2old^2 but actual self contribution old^2; for nonself min we set vals[i]=inf, so okay. For other p where k=2p? No, constraint for coordinate i with p≠i always two terms, correct.
For k such that k-i = j and j maybe equals i? only self.

If `old=0`, `vals = (1-dm)/(2y)` and self bound. Good.

After pass, `c` may have max slightly changed. We may want to `c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)`? Not now.

`normalize_to_max1(a,c)`:
```
def normalize_to_max1(a, c=None):
    if c is None:
       c=convolve_fft(np.asarray(a,float))
    m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300:
       return a*0.0, c
    factor = 1.0 / math.sqrt(m)
    a = np.asarray(a,float) * factor
    c = c * (factor * factor)
    # remove tiny negatives? no
    return a, c
```
If m<1, scale up. If m>1, down. This always targets M=1. At coordinate search start, if max conv from random is huge, factor small.

`local_score(a,c,n)`:
```
def local_score(a,c,n):
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if S <=0 or not finite: return inf
    # if m !=1, actual score = 2n*m/S^2; but if we normalize, m=1. Use exact local to be safe.
    return 2.0 * n * m / (S*S)
```
If m slightly below1, score lower? But if m<1, we can scale up, so use normalized m. After normalize, m≈1.

Let's refine `coordinate_search_for_n` with state and initial start from state if same n:
```
def coordinate_search_for_n(n, end_time, state, seed_val):
    rng_seed=seed_val
    rng=np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)
    starts=[]
    if state['seq'] is not None and len(state['seq'])==n:
       a=np.asarray(state['seq'],float); starts.append(a)
    # deterministic starts
    det_kinds=[0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7]
    # power grid starts
    for p in (-0.5,0.25,0.75,1.5):
       for q in (-0.5,0.25,0.75,1.5):
           starts.append(power_window_sequence(n,p,q))
           starts.append(base_power_window_sequence(n,p,q))
    # random starts
    for _ in range(min(40, max(10, n//2))):
       kind=int(rng.integers(0,20))
       starts.append(make_start(n,rng,kind))
    # normalize all? make_start already normalized. power sequences need normalize; define returns normalized.
    # Remove None
    starts=[s for s in starts if s is not None and np.any(s>0)]
    # Maybe sort by quick score? expensive. We can just put state first.
    best_local=None; best_local_score=inf
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
       if not starts:
          if best_local is not None:
             # generate a batch of perturbations and random
             for _ in range(5): starts.append(make_start(n,rng,20,best_local))
             for _ in range(10): starts.append(make_start(n,rng,int(rng.integers(0,20))))
          else:
             for _ in range(20): starts.append(make_start(n,rng,int(rng.integers(0,20))))
          continue
       a=starts.pop()
       a,c=normalize_to_max1(a)
       if a is None: continue
       slice_s = 0.05 if n <= 64 else (0.1 if n <= 128 else (0.2 if n <= 512 else 0.4))
       a,c=coordinate_optimize_slice(a,c,slice_s,end_time)
       if a is None: continue
       a,c=normalize_to_max1(a,c)
       S=float(np.sum(a)); m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
       score=2*n*m/(S*S) if S>0 else inf
       if math.isfinite(score):
          if score < best_local_score - 1e-9:
             best_local_score=score; best_local=a.copy(); no_improve=0
             if score < state['score'] - 1e-7:
                seq=sanitize_for_evaluate(best_local)
                sc=evaluate_sequence(seq)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < state['score']:
                    state['score']=sc; state['seq']=seq
                    print(f"update n={n} eval={sc:.6f}")
          else:
             no_improve += 1
             if no_improve > 30 and best_local is not None:
                 # force random restarts
                 starts=[]
                 no_improve=0
       if time.monotonic() >= end_time: break
```

Implement `coordinate_optimize_slice` with `passes=0`:
```
def coordinate_optimize_slice(a,c,max_time,deadline):
    t0=time.monotonic(); passes=0
    while time.monotonic() < min(t0+max_time, deadline):
        S_before=float(np.sum(a))
        coordinate_pass(a,c,False)
        passes +=1
        if passes % 16 ==0:
            a,c=normalize_to_max1(a) # recompute
            if a is None: break
        # maybe check time for second pass
        if time.monotonic() >= min(t0+max_time, deadline): break
        coordinate_pass(a,c,True)
        passes +=1
        if passes % 16 ==0:
            a,c=normalize_to_max1(a)
            if a is None: break
        S_after=float(np.sum(a))
        if S_after <= S_before*(1.0+1e-12):
            # maybe do a few more? break
            break
    a,c=normalize_to_max1(a,c)
    return a,c
```
If `normalize_to_max1(a)` recomputes c, pass_count? okay.

But if S_after <= S_before due to scaling down after infeasible, break may be premature. However coordinate should increase. Good.

Potential issue: `coordinate_pass` can cause c max >1 and then normalize scales down, S decreases below before. If happens often, break. But start feasible, coordinate bounds ensure no large violation. Rounding only.

Top-level code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

_SMALL = 1e-15
_EVAL_MARGIN = 0.99999999

def next_pow_two(m):
    p=1
    while p < m: p <<=1
    return p

def convolve_fft(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n ==0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if n <= 256:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    N=next_pow_two(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:2*n-1]
    # clean tiny imaginary? real already
    return c

def central_binomial(n):
    a=np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    if n<=0: return a
    val=1.0
    a[0]=val
    for k in range(1,n):
        val *= (2.0*k - 1.0)/(2.0*k)
        a[k]=val
    return a

def normalize_to_max1(a, c=None):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=float).copy()
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    if a.size ==0: return None,None
    if c is None:
        c=convolve_fft(a)
    else:
        c=np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    if c.size==0:
        return None,None
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= _SMALL:
        return None,None
    factor=1.0/math.sqrt(m)
    a=a*factor
    c=c*(factor*factor)
    return a,c
...
```
In `normalize_to_max1`, if c is view and we multiply `c=c*(...)`, new array; fine. If c length not 2n-1? But okay.

`sanitize_for_evaluate(a)`:
```
def sanitize_for_evaluate(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=float).copy()
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return []
    c=convolve_fft(a)
    m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if math.isfinite(m) and m > _SMALL:
        target = 1.0 - 1e-8
        a *= math.sqrt(target / m)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        a=np.ones(n, dtype=float)*(0.01/n)
    return [float(x) for x in a]
```
If m<target, scale up; if m>target, scale down. Good.

`raw_power_window`:
```
def raw_power_window(n, p, q, use_base):
    if n <=1: return np.ones(n)
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    left=(i+1.0)/float(n)
    right=(float(n) - i + 1.0)/float(n)  # i=0 -> (n+1? wait n - 0 +1 /n =1+1/n) too big.
```
Need right normalize: For i=0, want maybe n/n=1; for i=n-1, want 1/n. So `right=(float(n) - i)/float(n)` gives i=0 1, i=n-1 1/n. But for i=n? none. For i=0, (n)/n=1. Good. left=(i+1)/n: i=0 1/n, i=n-1 1. If we want symmetric endpoints high? Choose accordingly. left low at start, right low at end.
`right = (float(n) - i) / float(n)` gives at i=n-1: 1/n. Good.
```
loga = p*np.log(left) + q*np.log(right)
loga = np.clip(loga, -50, 50)
a=np.exp(loga)
if use_base:
    a *= central_binomial(n)
a=np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
return a
```
If p or q negative, left/right small positive, log negative, exp large up to e^50; normalize later.

`make_start`:
```
def make_start(n, rng, kind, best=None):
    if n <=0: return None
    if n ==1:
        return normalize... array([1.0])
    a=None
    if kind == 0: a=central_binomial(n)
    elif kind ==1: a=np.ones(n)
    elif kind ==2: beta=rng.uniform(0.2,5.0); a=np.exp(-beta*np.arange(n, dtype=float)/max(1,n-1))
    elif kind ==3: a=np.exp(beta*np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1))
    elif kind ==4:
        x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)/max(1,n-1)
        p=rng.uniform(0.5,3.0)
        a=np.sin(np.pi*x)**p + 1e-3
    elif kind ==5:
        x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)/max(1,n-1)
        p=rng.uniform(0.2,2.0)
        a=(0.5+0.5*np.cos(np.pi*x))**p + 1e-3  # endpoints high? cos(0)=1, cos(pi)=-1? cos(pi*x): x=1 -> -1 ->0. So endpoints 1 and0? asymmetric. Maybe use cos(2πx/2?) Simpler: (1 - |(2x-1)|)^p high center.
    elif kind ==6: p=rng.uniform(-1.0,2.0); q=rng.uniform(-1.0,2.0); a=raw_power_window(n,p,q,False)
    elif kind ==7: p=...; a=raw_power_window(n,p,q,True)
    elif kind ==8:
        sigma=rng.uniform(0.2,1.5)
        z=rng.normal(0.0,sigma,n)
        z=np.clip(z,-15,15)
        a=np.exp(z)
    elif kind ==9:
        mu=rng.uniform(0.05,0.95)*n
        sig=max(0.5, rng.uniform(0.1,0.6)*n)
        i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
        a=np.exp(-((i-mu)**2)/(2.0*sig*sig))
    elif kind ==10:
        p=rng.uniform(-1.0,2.0); q=rng.uniform(-1.0,2.0)
        a=raw_power_window(n,p,q,False)
        a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, rng.uniform(0.1,0.5), n))
    elif kind ==20 and best is not None:
        b=np.asarray(best, dtype=float)
        if b.size != n:
            b=b[:n] if b.size>n else np.pad(b,(0,n-b.size), mode='edge')
        sigma = float(rng.choice([0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0,1.5]))
        shift = int(rng.integers(-max(1,n//5), max(1,n//5))) if rng.random()<0.25 else 0
        if shift:
            b=np.roll(b, shift)
        noise=np.exp(np.clip(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,n), -5,5))
        a=b*noise
        if rng.random()<0.2:
            a=0.5*(a+a[::-1])
    else:
        a=np.ones(n)
    if a is None: return None
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.size != n:
        if a.size > n: a=a[:n]
        else: a=np.pad(a, (0, n-a.size), mode='edge')
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    if float(np.sum(a)) <= 0.0:
        a=np.ones(n)
    out,c=normalize_to_max1(a)
    return out
```
Kind 5 high center: use `a = (1.0 - np.abs(2.0*x - 1.0)) ** p + 1e-3` triangular low endpoints high center. Good.
Kind 2/3 geometric: for n=1 already. beta increasing could large exp (5) fine.

`update_global` top-level:
```
def update_global(state, seq):
    sc = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < state['score']:
        state['score']=float(sc)
        state['seq']=seq
        print(f"global score {state['score']:.9f} len {len(seq)}", flush=True)
```

`coordinate_search_for_n` with active:
```
def coordinate_search_for_n(n, end_time, state, seed_val):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed_val)
    starts=[]
    if state['seq'] is not None and len(state['seq']) == n:
        a=np.asarray(state['seq'], dtype=float)
        out,c=normalize_to_max1(a)
        if out is not None: starts.append(out)
    for kind in (0,1,2,3,4,5):
        a=make_start(n,rng,kind)
        if a is not None: starts.append(a)
    grid=(-0.5,0.5,1.5)
    for p in grid:
       for q in grid:
          out,c=normalize_to_max1(raw_power_window(n,p,q,False))
          if out is not None: starts.append(out)
          out,c=normalize_to_max1(raw_power_window(n,p,q,True))
          if out is not None: starts.append(out)
    generate_starts(starts,n,rng,None, min(20, max(6,n//4)))
    best_local=None; best_local_score=math.inf
    active=None; active_no=0
    max_continue = 4 if n <= 64 else (6 if n <= 256 else 8)
    slice_s = 0.05 if n <= 64 else (0.1 if n <= 256 else (0.2 if n <= 1024 else 0.5))
    starts_generated=0
    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
       if active is None:
          if len(starts) == 0:
             generate_starts(starts, n, rng, best_local, 20)
             if len(starts)==0: break
          a=starts.pop()
          a,c=normalize_to_max1(a)
          if a is None:
             continue
          active=[a,c]
          active_no=0
       a,c=coordinate_optimize_slice(active[0], active[1], slice_s, end_time)
       if a is None:
          active=None; continue
       a,c=normalize_to_max1(a,c)
       if a is None:
          active=None; continue
       S=float(np.sum(a)); m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
       score=math.inf
       if S > _SMALL and m > _SMALL and math.isfinite(S) and math.isfinite(m):
          score = 2.0 * n * m / (S*S)
       if math.isfinite(score):
          if score < best_local_score - 1e-10:
             best_local_score=score
             best_local=a.copy()
             active_no=0
             if score < state['score'] - 1e-7:
                seq=sanitize_for_evaluate(best_local)
                sc=evaluate_sequence(seq)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < state['score']:
                   state['score']=float(sc); state['seq']=seq
                   print(f"update n={n} eval={state['score']:.9f}", flush=True)
          else:
             active_no += 1
       else:
          active_no += 1
       if active_no >= max_continue:
          active=None
       # If starts exhausted and best good, generate more
       if len(starts)==0 and time.monotonic() < end_time:
          generate_starts(starts,n,rng,best_local,20)
    # at end, if best_local better than state score but not evaluated, eval
    if best_local is not None and best_local_score < state['score']:
       seq=sanitize_for_evaluate(best_local)
       sc=evaluate_sequence(seq)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < state['score']:
          state['score']=float(sc); state['seq']=seq
          print(...)
```
This will keep active for max_continue no-improvement slices. If a start improves, active_no reset and continues indefinitely until multiple no-improve. Good.

`coordinate_optimize_slice` with recompute:
```
def coordinate_optimize_slice(a,c,max_time,deadline):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    limit=min(t0+max_time, deadline)
    passes=0
    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        S_before=float(np.sum(a))
        coordinate_pass(a,c,False)
        passes +=1
        if passes % 16 ==0:
            a,c=normalize_to_max1(a)
            if a is None: break
        if time.monotonic() >= limit: break
        coordinate_pass(a,c,True)
        passes +=1
        if passes % 16 ==0:
            a,c=normalize_to_max1(a)
            if a is None: break
        S_after=float(np.sum(a))
        if S_after <= S_before*(1.0 + 1e-11):
            break
    a,c=normalize_to_max1(a,c)
    return a,c
```
If active start is already optimized and no improvement, this returns after one pass (or two?) after S no improve. Good.

But if S_before sum and after normalizing scale up, S_after may be >S_before. If coordinate pass no change but m<1 (shouldn't after previous normalize), scale up increases S and no_improvement? It will see improvement and continue maybe. But normalize to M=1 after each slice, so m=1.

```python
"""
Search non-negative coefficient sequences minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

Main ideas:
- Normalize candidates so that max(convolve(a, a)) = 1.  Then the objective
  is just 2n / sum(a)^2, so maximizing the sum under the convolution peak
  constraint is ideal.
- Use a coordinate-maximization pass: for each coordinate a_i, all other
  coordinates fixed, compute the largest feasible a_i while keeping every
  convolution coefficient <= 1.  This is O(n) per coordinate using the
  maintained full convolution.
- Explore many structured and random starting shapes, including truncated
  central-binomial sequences, power windows, Gaussians, and multiplicative
  perturbations of the current best shape.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

_SMALL = 1e-15
_FINAL_MARGIN = 0.99999999


def next_pow_two(m):
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def convolve_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 256:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    N = next_pow_two(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    return c


def central_binomial(n):
    a = np.empty(max(0, n), dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return a
    v = 1.0
    a[0] = v
    for k in range(1, n):
        v *= (2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k)
        a[k] = v
    return a


def normalize_to_max1(a, c=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None, None
    if c is None:
        c = convolve_fft(a)
    else:
        c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if c.size == 0:
        return None, None
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= _SMALL:
        return None, None
    f = 1.0 / math.sqrt(m)
    a = a * f
    c = c * (f * f)
    return a, c


def sanitize_for_evaluate(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return []
    c = convolve_fft(a)
    m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if math.isfinite(m) and m > _SMALL:
        a *= math.sqrt(_FINAL_MARGIN / m)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * (0.01 / n)
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def raw_power_window(n, p, q, use_base):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    left = (i + 1.0) / float(n)
    right = (float(n) - i) / float(n)
    loga = p * np.log(left) + q * np.log(right)
    loga = np.clip(loga, -50.0, 50.0)
    a = np.exp(loga)
    if use_base:
        a *= central_binomial(n)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    return a


def make_start(n, rng, kind, best=None):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if n == 1:
        out, c = normalize_to_max1(np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64))
        return out

    a = None
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = i / float(n - 1)

    if kind == 0:
        a = central_binomial(n)
    elif kind == 1:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif kind == 2:
        beta = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 5.0))
        a = np.exp(-beta * i / float(n - 1))
    elif kind == 3:
        beta = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 5.0))
        a = np.exp(beta * i / float(n - 1))
    elif kind == 4:
        p = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 3.0))
        a = (0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)) ** p + 1e-3
    elif kind == 5:
        p = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 3.0))
        a = (1.0 - np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)) ** p + 1e-3
    elif kind == 6:
        p = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 2.0))
        q = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 2.0))
        a = raw_power_window(n, p, q, False)
    elif kind == 7:
        p = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 2.0))
        q = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 2.0))
        a = raw_power_window(n, p, q, True)
    elif kind == 8:
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.5))
        z = np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n), -15.0, 15.0)
        a = np.exp(z)
    elif kind == 9:
        mu = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.95)) * float(n)
        sig = max(0.5, float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.6)) * float(n))
        a = np.exp(-((i - mu) * (i - mu)) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
    elif kind == 10:
        p = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 2.0))
        q = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 2.0))
        a = raw_power_window(n, p, q, False)
        noise = np.exp(np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.5)), n), -5.0, 5.0))
        a = a * noise
    elif kind >= 20 and best is not None:
        b = np.asarray(best, dtype=np.float64)
        if b.size != n:
            if b.size > n:
                b = b[:n]
            else:
                b = np.pad(b, (0, n - b.size), mode="edge")
        sigma = float(rng.choice([0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0, 1.5]))
        if rng.random() < 0.25:
            shift = int(rng.integers(-max(1, n // 5), max(1, n // 5)))
            b = np.roll(b, shift)
        noise = np.exp(np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n), -5.0, 5.0))
        a = b * noise
        if rng.random() < 0.2:
            a = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if a is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size != n:
        if a.size > n:
            a = a[:n]
        else:
            a = np.pad(a, (0, n - a.size), mode="edge")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(np.sum(a)) <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out, c = normalize_to_max1(a)
    return out


def generate_starts(starts, n, rng, best_local, count):
    for _ in range(count):
        if best_local is not None and rng.random() < 0.5:
            a = make_start(n, rng, 20, best_local)
        else:
            kind = int(rng.integers(0, 20))
            a = make_start(n, rng, kind)
        if a is not None:
            starts.append(a)


def coordinate_pass(a, c, reverse=False):
    n = a.size
    order = range(n - 1, -1, -1) if reverse else range(n)

    for idx in order:
        old = float(a[idx])
        if old < 0.0:
            old = 0.0
            a[idx] = 0.0

        y = a
        c_slice = c[idx:idx + n]
        dm = c_slice - 2.0 * old * y

        with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            vals = (1.0 - dm) / (2.0 * y)

        mask = y > _SMALL
        if not np.all(mask):
            vals[~mask] = np.inf
        vals[idx] = np.inf

        nonself_min = float(np.min(vals))

        kself = 2 * idx
        dself = float(c[kself]) - old * old
        if dself <= 1.0:
            self_min = math.sqrt(max(0.0, 1.0 - dself))
        else:
            self_min = 0.0

        xmax = nonself_min if nonself_min < self_min else self_min
        if not math.isfinite(xmax):
            xmax = old

        if xmax < 0.0:
            if old > 0.0 and (old - xmax) <= 1e-9 * max(1.0, old):
                xmax = old
            else:
                xmax = 0.0
        if xmax > 1.0:
            xmax = 1.0

        new = xmax
        if new > old + 1e-14:
            delta = new - old
            update = 2.0 * delta * a
            update[idx] = new * new - old * old
            c[idx:idx + n] += update
            a[idx] = new
        elif old - new > 1e-9:
            delta = new - old
            update = 2.0 * delta * a
            update[idx] = new * new - old * old
            c[idx:idx + n] += update
            a[idx] = new


def coordinate_optimize_slice(a, c, max_time, deadline):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    limit = min(t0 + float(max_time), float(deadline))
    passes = 0

    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        s_before = float(np.sum(a))
        coordinate_pass(a, c, False)
        passes += 1
        if passes % 16 == 0:
            a, c = normalize_to_max1(a)
            if a is None:
                break

        if time.monotonic() >= limit:
            break

        coordinate_pass(a, c, True)
        passes += 1
        if passes % 16 == 0:
            a, c = normalize_to_max1(a)
            if a is None:
                break

        s_after = float(np.sum(a))
        if not math.isfinite(s_after):
            break
        if s_after <= s_before * (1.0 + 1e-11):
            break

    if a is not None:
        a, c = normalize_to_max1(a, c)
    return a, c


def update_global(state, seq):
    sc = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and float(sc) < state["score"]:
        state["score"] = float(sc)
        state["seq"] = seq
        print(f"global update: score={state['score']:.9f} len={len(seq)}", flush=True)


def coordinate_search_for_n(n, end_time, state, seed_val):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed_val)
    starts = []

    if state["seq"] is not None and len(state["seq"]) == n:
        out, c = normalize_to_max1(state["seq"])
        if out is not None:
            starts.append(out)

    for kind in (0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5):
        a = make_start(n, rng, kind)
        if a is not None:
            starts.append(a)

    grid = (-0.5, 0.5, 1.5)
    for p in grid:
        for q in grid:
            out, c = normalize_to_max1(raw_power_window(n, p, q, False))
            if out is not None:
                starts.append(out)
            out, c = normalize_to_max1(raw_power_window(n, p, q, True))
            if out is not None:
                starts.append(out)

    generate_starts(starts, n, rng, None, min(24, max(8, n // 4)))

    best_local = None
    best_local_score = math.inf
    active = None
    active_no_improve = 0

    if n <= 64:
        slice_s = 0.05
        max_continue = 5
    elif n <= 256:
        slice_s = 0.10
        max_continue = 6
    elif n <= 1024:
        slice_s = 0.20
        max_continue = 7
    else:
        slice_s = 0.45
        max_continue = 3

    while time.monotonic() < float(end_time):
        if active is None:
            if not starts:
                generate_starts(starts, n, rng, best_local, 20)
                if not starts:
                    break
            a = starts.pop()
            a, c = normalize_to_max1(a)
            if a is None:
                continue
            active = [a, c]
            active_no_improve = 0

        a, c = coordinate_optimize_slice(active[0], active[1], slice_s, end_time)
        if a is None:
            active = None
            continue
        a, c = normalize_to_max1(a, c)
        if a is None:
            active = None
            continue

        s = float(np.sum(a))
        m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        score = math.inf
        if s > _SMALL and m > _SMALL and math.isfinite(s) and math.isfinite(m):
            if m < 1.0:
                m = 1.0
            elif m < 1.0 + 1e-6:
                m = 1.0
            score = 2.0 * float(n) * m / (s * s)

        if math.isfinite(score):
            if score < best_local_score - 1e-10:
                best_local_score = score
                best_local = a.copy()
                active_no_improve = 0
                if score < state["score"] - 1e-7:
                    seq = sanitize_for_evaluate(best_local)
                    sc = evaluate_sequence(seq)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and float(sc) < state["score"]:
                        state["score"] = float(sc)
                        state["seq"] = seq
                        print(f"n={n} eval update: score={state['score']:.9f}", flush=True)
            else:
                active_no_improve += 1
        else:
            active_no_improve += 1

        if active_no_improve >= max_continue:
            active = None

        if not starts and time.monotonic() < float(end_time):
            generate_starts(starts, n, rng, best_local, 20)

    if best_local is not None and best_local_score < state["score"]:
        seq = sanitize_for_evaluate(best_local)
        sc = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and float(sc) < state["score"]:
            state["score"] = float(sc)
            state["seq"] = seq
            print(f"n={n} final local eval: score={state['score']:.9f}", flush=True)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    state = {"score": math.inf, "seq": None}

    print(f"starting: seed={seed} budget={budget_s}", flush=True)

    # Optional parent sequence: evaluate as a valid fallback / reference.
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except NameError:
        parent = None
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and parent.ndim == 1 and parent.size > 0:
        parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(parent)) and float(np.sum(parent)) > 0.01:
            seq = sanitize_for_evaluate(parent)
            update_global(state, seq)

    # Reliable non-trivial baseline: truncated central-binomial sequence.
    a = central_binomial(1024)
    a, c = normalize_to_max1(a)
    if a is not None:
        update_global(state, sanitize_for_evaluate(a))
    else:
        a = central_binomial(64)
        a, c = normalize_to_max1(a)
        if a is not None:
            update_global(state, sanitize_for_evaluate(a))

    if state["seq"] is None:
        a = central_binomial(64)
        a, c = normalize_to_max1(a)
        seq = sanitize_for_evaluate(a) if a is not None else [1.0]
        update_global(state, seq)

    specs = [
        (8, 15),
        (12, 15),
        (16, 25),
        (24, 35),
        (32, 45),
        (48, 55),
        (64, 75),
        (96, 90),
        (128, 110),
        (192, 130),
        (256, 150),
        (384, 160),
        (512, 170),
        (768, 150),
        (1024, 160),
    ]

    total_budget = float(sum(b for _, b in specs))
    available = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic() - 30.0)
    scale = min(1.0, available / total_budget) if total_budget > 0 else 0.0

    for n, budget in specs:
        if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline) - 15.0:
            break
        use = float(budget) * scale
        if use < 3.0:
            break
        end = time.monotonic() + min(use, max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic() - 10.0))
        coordinate_search_for_n(n, end, state, int(seed) + n * 1000)
        print(
            f"search n={n} done, best={state['score']:.9f}, t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s",
            flush=True
        )

    # Final polish on the current best length if it is small enough.
    if state["seq"] is not None:
        nb = len(state["seq"])
        if 2 <= nb <= 1024 and time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 10.0:
            end = float(deadline) - 2.0
            coordinate_search_for_n(nb, end, state, int(seed) + 987654)

    if state["seq"] is None:
        a = central_binomial(128)
        a, c = normalize_to_max1(a)
        state["seq"] = sanitize_for_evaluate(a) if a is not None else [1.0]
        update_global(state, state["seq"])
    else:
        final_seq = sanitize_for_evaluate(state["seq"])
        sc = evaluate_sequence(final_seq)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and float(sc) <= state["score"] + 1e-12:
            state["seq"] = final_seq
            state["score"] = float(sc)
        else:
            a = central_binomial(128)
            a, c = normalize_to_max1(a)
            state["seq"] = sanitize_for_evaluate(a) if a is not None else [1.0]
            update_global(state, state["seq"])

    print(f"final score={state['score']:.9f} len={len(state['seq'])}", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in state["seq"]]
```
